De incertitudine et vanitate scientiarum (1564, Latin)
======================================================
Heinrich Cornelius Agrippa von Nettesheim — De incertitudine et vanitate scientiarum et artium (On the Uncertainty and Vanity of the Sciences), 1564 Latin edition, public domain. Agrippa's skeptical counterpart to De occulta philosophia. Scan via Internet Archive (Wellcome Trust copy).
